                    if (!nav || nav.dataset.scrolled) return;

                    if (!active) return;
                    var navRect = nav.getBoundingClientRect();

style="color:var(--shiki-token-keyword)">default</span><span style="color:var(--shiki-color-text)"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:var(--shiki-color-text)">  </span><span style="color:var(--shiki-token-function)">fetch</span><span style="color:var(--shiki-color-text)">(request</span><span style="color:var(--shiki-token-keyword)">:</span><span style="color:var(--shiki-color-text)"> </span><span style="color:var(--shiki-token-function)">Request</span><span style="color:var(--shiki-color-text)">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:var(--shiki-color-text)">    </span><span style="color:var(--shiki-token-keyword)">return</span><span style="color:var(--shiki-color-text)"> </span><span style="color:var(--shiki-token-keyword)">new</span><span style="color:var(--shiki-color-text)"> </span><span style="color:var(--shiki-token-function)">Response</span><span style="color:var(--shiki-color-text)">(</span><span style="color:var(--shiki-token-string-expression)">&#x27;Hello from Vercel!&#x27;</span><span style="color:var(--shiki-color-text)">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:var(--shiki-color-text)">  }</span><span style="color:var(--shiki-token-punctuation)">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:var(--shiki-color-text)">};</span></span></code></pre></div></div><!--/$--></div></section>
<p class="my-4">When you deploy your application, Vercel automatically sets up the tools and optimizations for your chosen <a data-zone="same" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-inherit transition-opacity duration-100 underline font-medium [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:opacity-60" href="/docs/frameworks">framework</a>. It ensures low latency by routing traffic through Vercel&#x27;s <a data-zone="same" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-inherit transition-opacity duration-100 underline font-medium [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:opacity-60" href="/docs/cdn">CDN</a>, and placing your functions in a specific region when you need more control over <a data-zone="same" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-inherit transition-opacity duration-100 underline font-medium [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:opacity-60" href="/docs/functions#functions-and-your-data-source">data locality</a>.</p>
<figure class="m-0 flex flex-col items-center" style="margin-block:40px"><img alt="Functions location within Vercel&#x27;s managed infrastructure" loading="lazy" width="1920" height="436" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" class="h-auto! max-w-full rounded-(--geist-marketing-radius) border border-solid border-(--accents-2) object-cover dark:hidden" style="color:transparent" srcSet="/vc-ap-vercel-docs/_next/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F7nyt0uhk7sse4zvn.public.blob.vercel-storage.com%2Ffront%2Fdocs%2Fvercel-functions%2Ffirst_image_light.png&amp;w=1920&amp;q=75 1x, /vc-ap-vercel-docs/_next/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F7nyt0uhk7sse4zvn.public.blob.vercel-storage.com%2Ffront%2Fdocs%2Fvercel-functions%2Ffirst_image_light.png&amp;w=3840&amp;q=75 2x" src="/vc-ap-vercel-docs/_next/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F7nyt0uhk7sse4zvn.public.blob.vercel-storage.com%2Ffront%2Fdocs%2Fvercel-functions%2Ffirst_image_light.png&amp;w=3840&amp;q=75"/><img alt="Functions location within Vercel&#x27;s managed infrastructure" loading="lazy" width="1920" height="436" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" class="hidden h-auto! max-w-full! rounded-(--geist-marketing-radius) border border-solid border-(--accents-2) object-cover dark:block" style="color:transparent" srcSet="/vc-ap-vercel-docs/_next/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F7nyt0uhk7sse4zvn.public.blob.vercel-storage.com%2Ffront%2Fdocs%2Fvercel-functions%2Ffirst_image_dark.png&amp;w=1920&amp;q=75 1x, /vc-ap-vercel-docs/_next/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F7nyt0uhk7sse4zvn.public.blob.vercel-storage.com%2Ffront%2Fdocs%2Fvercel-functions%2Ffirst_image_dark.png&amp;w=3840&amp;q=75 2x" src="/vc-ap-vercel-docs/_next/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F7nyt0uhk7sse4zvn.public.blob.vercel-storage.com%2Ffront%2Fdocs%2Fvercel-functions%2Ffirst_image_dark.png&amp;w=3840&amp;q=75"/><figcaption class="mt-3 text-center text-[14px] text-(--accents-5)">Functions location within Vercel&#x27;s managed infrastructure</figcaption></figure>
<div class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__container"><h2 class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__header text-heading-20 sm:text-heading-24" data-components-heading="true"><span class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__target" id="what-vercel-functions-provide"></span><a class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__title [&amp;&gt;p]:m-0" href="#what-vercel-functions-provide">What Vercel Functions provide</a><a href="#what-vercel-functions-provide" class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__permalink" aria-label="Copy link to section"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor" class="text-[0.6em]"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></a></h2></div>
<ul class="my-3 list-disc pl-6 [&amp;&gt;li::marker]:text-gray-500">
<li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0"><span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Zero server management.</span> Deploy code that scales automatically with traffic</li>
<li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0"><span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Fluid compute.</span> Reduced cold starts, lower latency, and lower costs via optimized concurrency</li>
<li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0"><span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Multi-runtime support.</span> Use Node.js, Python, Go, and more</li>
<li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0"><span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Regional control.</span> Pin functions to a region close to your data source</li>
<li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0"><span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Framework-aware.</span> Vercel detects and optimizes for your framework automatically</li>
<li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0"><span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Built-in observability.</span> View invocations, errors, and cost metrics in the dashboard</li>
</ul>
<div class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__container"><h2 class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__header text-heading-20 sm:text-heading-24" data-components-heading="true"><span class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__target" id="create-your-first-vercel-function"></span><a class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__title [&amp;&gt;p]:m-0" href="#create-your-first-vercel-function">Create your first Vercel Function</a><a href="#create-your-first-vercel-function" class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__permalink" aria-label="Copy link to section"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor" class="text-[0.6em]"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></a></h2></div>

<p class="my-4">To learn more, see the <a data-zone="same" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-inherit transition-opacity duration-100 underline font-medium [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:opacity-60" href="/docs/functions/quickstart">quickstart</a> or <a href="/templates" data-zone="91c5f9" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-inherit transition-opacity duration-100 underline font-medium [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:opacity-60">deploy a template</a>.</p>
<div class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__container"><h2 class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__header text-heading-20 sm:text-heading-24" data-components-heading="true"><span class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__target" id="how-the-vercel-functions-lifecycle-works"></span><a class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__title [&amp;&gt;p]:m-0" href="#how-the-vercel-functions-lifecycle-works">How the Vercel Functions lifecycle works</a><a href="#how-the-vercel-functions-lifecycle-works" class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__permalink" aria-label="Copy link to section"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor" class="text-[0.6em]"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></a></h2></div>
<p class="my-4">Vercel Functions handle each incoming request as a new invocation, scaling automatically so you don&#x27;t need to manage servers. They run in a single <a data-zone="same" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-inherit transition-opacity duration-100 underline font-medium [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:opacity-60" href="/docs/functions/configuring-functions/region">region</a> by default, although you can configure them to run in multiple regions if you have globally replicated data. These functions let you add extra capabilities to your application, such as handling authentication, streaming data, or querying databases.</p>
<p class="my-4">When a user sends a request to your site, Vercel can automatically run a function based on your application code. You don&#x27;t need to manage servers or handle scaling.</p>
<p class="my-4">Vercel creates a new function invocation for each incoming request. If another request arrives soon after the previous one, Vercel <a data-zone="same" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-inherit transition-opacity duration-100 underline font-medium [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:opacity-60" href="/docs/fluid-compute#optimized-concurrency">reuses the same function</a> instance to optimize performance and cost efficiency. Over time, Vercel only keeps as many active functions as needed to handle your traffic. Vercel scales your functions down to zero when there are no incoming requests.</p>
<p class="my-4">By allowing concurrent execution within the same instance (and so using idle time for compute), fluid compute reduces cold starts, lowers latency, and saves on compute costs. It also prevents the need to spin up multiple isolated instances when tasks spend most of their time waiting for external operations.</p>
<div class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__container"><h3 class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__header text-heading-16 sm:text-heading-18" data-components-heading="true"><span class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__target" id="why-functions-should-run-near-your-data-source"></span><a class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__title [&amp;&gt;p]:m-0" href="#why-functions-should-run-near-your-data-source">Why functions should run near your data source</a><a href="#why-functions-should-run-near-your-data-source" class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__permalink" aria-label="Copy link to section"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor" class="text-[0.6em]"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></a></h3></div>
<p class="my-4">Functions should always execute close to where your data source is to reduce latency. By default, functions using the Node.js runtime execute in Washington, D.C., USA (<code class="code-module__V4tPXG__code code-module__V4tPXG__inlineCode">iad1</code>), a common location for external data sources. You can set a new region through your <a data-zone="same" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-inherit transition-opacity duration-100 underline font-medium [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:opacity-60" href="/docs/functions/configuring-functions/region#setting-your-default-region">project&#x27;s settings on Vercel</a>.</p>
<div class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__container"><h2 class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__header text-heading-20 sm:text-heading-24" data-components-heading="true"><span class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__target" id="viewing-vercel-function-metrics"></span><a class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__title [&amp;&gt;p]:m-0" href="#viewing-vercel-function-metrics">Viewing Vercel Function metrics</a><a href="#viewing-vercel-function-metrics" class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__permalink" aria-label="Copy link to section"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor" class="text-[0.6em]"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></a></h2></div>
<p class="my-4">You can view various performance and cost efficiency metrics using Vercel Observability:</p>
<ol class="my-3 list-decimal pl-6">
<li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">Choose your project from the <a href="https://vercel.com/d?to=%2F%5Bteam%5D%2F%5Bproject%5D&amp;title=Go+to+dashboard" rel="noopener" target="_blank" data-zone="null" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-inherit transition-opacity duration-100 underline font-medium [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:opacity-60">dashboard</a>.</li>
<li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">Click on the <span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Observability</span> tab and select the <span class="strong-module__WGWgwq__docsString">Vercel Functions</span> section.</li>
<li class="mb-2 pl-1 leading-[1.7] [&amp;&gt;svg]:inline-flex last:mb-0">Click on the chevron icon to expand and see all charts.</li>
</ol>
<p class="my-4">You&#x27;ll see current function usage metrics, including Active CPU, Provisioned Memory, Invocations, and savings from the <a data-zone="same" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-inherit transition-opacity duration-100 underline font-medium [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:opacity-60" href="/docs/fluid-compute#optimized-concurrency">optimized concurrency model</a> when fluid compute is enabled.</p>
<div class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__container"><h2 class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__header text-heading-20 sm:text-heading-24" data-components-heading="true"><span class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__target" id="how-vercel-functions-pricing-works"></span><a class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__title [&amp;&gt;p]:m-0" href="#how-vercel-functions-pricing-works">How Vercel Functions pricing works</a><a href="#how-vercel-functions-pricing-works" class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__permalink" aria-label="Copy link to section"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor" class="text-[0.6em]"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></a></h2></div>
<p class="my-4">Vercel Functions are priced based on active CPU, provisioned memory, and invocations. See the <a data-zone="same" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-inherit transition-opacity duration-100 underline font-medium [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:opacity-60" href="/docs/functions/usage-and-pricing">fluid compute pricing</a> documentation for more information.</p>
<p class="my-4">If your project is not using fluid compute, see the <a data-zone="same" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 text-inherit transition-opacity duration-100 underline font-medium [:not([aria-disabled])]:hover:opacity-60" href="/docs/functions/usage-and-pricing/legacy-pricing">legacy pricing documentation</a> for Vercel Functions.</p>
<div class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__container"><h2 class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__header text-heading-20 sm:text-heading-24" data-components-heading="true"><span class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__target" id="explore-vercel-functions"></span><a class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__title [&amp;&gt;p]:m-0" href="#explore-vercel-functions">Explore Vercel Functions</a><a href="#explore-vercel-functions" class="linked-heading-module__rvMliW__permalink" aria-label="Copy link to section"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="16" width="16" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor" class="text-[0.6em]"><path fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M8.47 1.47a4.29 4.29 0 1 1 6.06 6.06l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06 2.5-2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 0-3.94-3.94l-2.5 2.5-1.06-1.06zm3.06 4.06-6 6-1.06-1.06 6-6zm-10.06 9a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 6.06 0l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a2.79 2.79 0 0 1-3.94-3.94l2.5-2.5-1.06-1.06-2.5 2.5a4.3 4.3 0 0 0 0 6.06" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></a></h2></div>
<div class="card-grid-module__tzmuLG__container"><a data-zone="same" class="cursor-pointer focus-visible:outline-2 outline-[var(--ds-focus-color)] outline-offset-4 card-grid-module__tzmuLG__card" href="/docs/fundamentals/what-is-compute"><div class="card-grid-module__tzmuLG__icon"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" height="24" width="24" data-slot="geist-icon" style="color:currentColor"><path
